3. Hybrid: start binary local search, then relax values (fractional) near 0/1 to smooth peaks, using SLSQP.
4. Parameterized structure: h_i = σ(W_i) where W_i is low-dimensional latent (Fourier, wavelet) then optimize coefficients. Could escape high-dimensional local minima.
5. Evolutionary/crossover: maintain population binary sequences, minimize max distance; use bit flips, block moves.

Idea: Let A be set {i: floor(α i) - 2 floor(β i)?} Beatty sequences? We need distance distribution between A and B nearly uniform. Difference sets of low-discrepancy sequences (e.g., van der Corput) may have nearly uniform pair distances. A and B as first half of ordered points by a permutation? Let's model continuous: choose sets A,B each measure1 in [0,2]. For each pair (a∈A,b∈B), distance |a-b|. We want all distance bins ≤T. If we can make the function mapping pairs to distances uniform. This is like two measure-preserving transformations. A and B can be interleaved uniformly. A simple construction: Let θ be golden ratio permutation: assign each point x to A if {α x} < 1/2, B otherwise, with α irrational. Then pairs (x,y) have (αx,αy) distribution? For x,y independent uniform, αx,αy are not independent but jointly equidistributed on torus for linear? The map (x,y)->(αx,αy) is linear with determinant α^2, on [0,2]^2 (mod1) equidistributed. Condition x∈A based on αx mod1<1/2, y∈B based on αy mod1≥1/2. Then the distance distribution between A and B might be close to uniform? For finite n, use sequence h_i=1 if fractional part(i*α) < 0.5. Let's analyze. For large n, for a fixed shift d, as i varies, (θ_i, θ_{i+d}) = (u, u+α d mod1), a line on torus. We need f_d = count u in set {iα mod1} where u<0.5 and u+δ≥0.5. For a random u, probability=1/4. But line may have nonuniform crossing: count of u in [0,0.5)∩[0.5-δ,1-δ) etc. For a low-discrepancy sequence u_i = iα mod1, the counter f_d = N - number of points of u in a union of intervals related to δ. Specifically f_d = # {i: u_i < 0.5, u_i+δ mod1 ≥0.5}. This = #u in [0.5,?]. Let δ=αd mod1. If 0<δ<0.5: condition u<0.5 and u+δ≥0.5 (not wrap) => u∈[0.5-δ,0.5): length δ. Also wrap? if u+δ≥1? u≥1-δ, but u<0.5 impossible if δ<0.5. So f_d ≈ (1/N) sum over u_i in interval length δ. Since u_i low discrepancy, count ≈ (n/2) δ? Wait f_d for one direction counts only ordered pairs at shift d? For h_i A (u<0.5), h_{i+d} B (u+δ≥0.5). Fraction among i is length of interval /1 = δ? But A probability 0.5; interval length δ (if δ<0.5) entirely within A half, so count ≈ n δ. For d with αd mod1 near 0.5, f_d≈0.5n -> C≈1! Bad. If δ uniform, average f_d? Over d, δ uniform, average length? For 0<δ<0.5 length δ; for δ>0.5? interval length 1-δ? average 0.25, f≈0.25n=48 for n=192. But max near 0.5n, bad. So one-frequency threshold not good.

Another approach: choose A as a "perfect hash" via greedy: For each pair distance, need capacity T. This is like we want a binary matrix with row sums? The pair count f_d = ∑_{i} x_i(1-x_{i+d}). We can express in terms of x vector and shift matrix. Need max ≤T. Greedy construction: start x all zeros, add m ones one by one, choosing position that increases distance frequencies least. Since total constraints m positions. At each add one at position p, it increases directed distance counts f_d for d = p - b for zeros b (to right? positive) and d = b - p? Actually adding 1 at p: For each existing zero b, it contributes: if b > p, distance d=b-p (from one p to zero b) increases f_{b-p}; if b < p, distance d=p-b increases f_{p-b}. So adding a one creates distances to previous zeros. Later zeros? We can view constructing A, complement B. New one at p will have cross distances to all non-A positions (including future zeros). It also removes cross distances from previous A to p (p was zero now one). So objective changes: for each existing A a, if a<p remove distance p-a from f? Previously pair (a,p) with one at a, zero p counted. Now both A no count. For each future zero not set, distances to A are potential. Hard.

Another combinatorial formulation: For binary x, f_d = ∑ x_i(1-x_{i+d}). This is quadratic: f_d = ∑_{i<j} [x_i(1-x_j) 1_{j-i=d} + x_j(1-x_i)1_{i-j=d?}]. We can use local search: flip a zero to one and one to zero (exchange) to maintain m. Exchange effect on f_d can be computed efficiently. We can accept moves that reduce max or peaks.

Implementation:
```
g = zeros(n)
if s>=0:
  # source j 0..n-1-s: term h[j+s]*(1-h[j]); derivative wrt h[j+s] += (1-h[j]); wrt h[j] -= h[j+s]
  # target i=j+s: i from s..n-1
  g[s:] += (1 - h[:-s]) if s>0 else (1-h)
  # source i=0..n-1-s
  g[:n-s] -= h[s:] if s>0 else h
else:
  d=-s
  # C[-d]=∑_{j=d}^{n-1} h[j-d](1-h[j])? Wait formula C[s]=∑ h[j+s](1-h[j]) with s negative. Let d=-s. j from d to n-1 (so j+s>=0). term h[j-d](1-h[j]). derivative: wrt h[j-d] += (1-h[j]); wrt h[j] -= h[j-d]. Target i=j-d (0..n-1-d): g[:n-d] += (1 - h[d:]); source i=j (d..n-1): g[d:] -= h[:n-d].
```

Let's ensure delta function is correct and efficient. Pseudocode top-level:
```
def corr_delta(h, p, q, t):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2*n - 1
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    # A: source delta at p and q
    rev_one = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    # r=p, dp=-t
    delta[p:p+n] += -t * rev_one
    # r=q, dp=+t
    delta[q:q+n] += t * rev_one
    # B: target delta at p and q
    # h forward
    start_p = n - 1 - p
    delta[start_p:start_p+n] += -t * h
    start_q = n - 1 - q
    delta[start_q:start_q+n] += t * h
    # D
    idx_mid = n-1
    delta[idx_mid] += 2*t*t
    idx1 = idx_mid + (q - p)
    idx2 = idx_mid - (q - p)
    delta[idx1] -= t*t
    delta[idx2] -= t*t
    return delta
```

Let's re-derive delta carefully. C(s) = ∑_j a_j b_j where a_j = h[j+s], b_j=1-h[j]. Let src index u=j+s, tgt/source? term h[u]*(1-h[j]) with u=j+s.
Change δ_u = -t at u=p, +t at u=q (p donor, q receiver). We update both src and tgt inside sum.
New-old for term j:
old = h[u](1-h[j]). new = (h[u]+δ_u)(1 - (h[j]+δ_j)).
= old + δ_u(1-h[j]) - h[u]δ_j - δ_u δ_j.
Sum over j. δ_u nonzero if u∈{p,q}; δ_j nonzero if j∈{p,q}.
Thus:
A(s) = sum_{u∈{p,q}} δ_u (1-h[u-s]) where j=u-s valid. (u source shifted)
B(s) = sum_{j∈{p,q}} h[j+s] δ_j. (j target/source index in (1-h))
D(s) = sum_{u∈{p,q}} sum_{j∈{p,q}}? Actually δ_uδ_j for same term requires u=j+s. So δ_u appears as source and δ_v as target for term j=v, u=v+s. So D(s)=Σ_v δ_{v+s}δ_v. Same as before.
Now map A. For u=r, contribution δ_r(1-h[r-s]) for valid j=r-s in [0,n-1] => s=r-j. Output index idx = s+n-1 = r-j+n-1. As j=0..n-1, idx = r+n-1, r+n-2, ..., r. So slice [r, r+n] (idx r..r+n-1) values for j=n-1 down to 0: (1-h[n-1]) ... (1-h[0]) = rev_one. That's what we did. Thus final delta code:
```
delta = zeros
rev_one = (1-h)[::-1]
# A: add δ_p * rev_one at slice p:p+n ; δ_q at q:q+n
delta[p:p+n] += -t * rev_one
delta[q:q+n] += +t * rev_one
# B subtract h[j]δ_j: add -δ_p*h at start_p, -δ_q*h at start_q
start=n-1-p
delta[start:start+n] += +t * h  # since -δ_p=t
start=n-1-q
delta[start:start+n] += -t * h
# D subtract Dpoints:
idx_mid += -2*t*t
idx_qp = idx_mid + (q-p): D = δ_q? For s=q-p: D=δ_q? Let's recompute Dpoint: D(s) = Σ_j δ[j+s]δ[j]
For s=q-p: j=p -> δ[q]δ[p]= -t^2. For s=p-q: j=q -> δ[p]δ[q]=-t^2.
So D = -t^2 at both diffs. Subtract D => +t^2 at both diffs.
mid D = δ_p^2+δ_q^2 = 2t^2, subtract => -2t^2.
```
Yes.

This function can evaluate trial delta quickly. We can avoid full C+delta if only need max. But we can compute:
```
trial = C + delta

```
Allocating trial each candidate. We can also update in place after best candidate. Candidate evaluation O(n) allocations. Fine.

Now choose moves. To reduce active peaks, sample from active lags and terms where current term high. But for continuous h, term values array can be computed? For a given s, terms over j valid: term = h[j+s]*(1-h[j]). We can pick a random high term. For active top A lags, we can vectorize high-term selection:
For each s in active list:
  valid j range: if s>=0: j=0..n-1-s; target=j+s; term = h[target]*(1-h[j]). If s<0: j=-s..n-1; target=j+s; term same.
To sample, we can just choose random s probability proportional to C[s]^α, then choose random term index uniformly among valid, maybe biased by term. If term is low, move may not help. Use probability proportional to term? We can approximate by random. For active s, choose j uniformly; p=target (decrease), q=source (increase). But if h[target] low and h[source] high, C term small; moving target to source not beneficial. Instead for each term, the direction to reduce term is if h_source < h_target? Because term high when target high, source low. Then moving from target to source reduces. If h_source > h_target, the term is low but other lags may be affected? Maybe not. We can choose direction based on pair: If we choose pair (u,v) with an active s such that v = u+s (target/source), and h[v] > h[u], set p=v (donor), q=u (receiver). Else if h[u] > h[v], then term low but moving from u to v increases this term but may reduce reverse lag? Since C includes both s and -s? If h_source>h_target, for same pair, term for opposite lag? C[-s] has source=target? For pair (u source, v target) with h_u>h_v, the complementary term for shift -s? C[-s] includes target? C[-s] = ∑ h[j-s](1-h[j]); for j=v target? not. Let's check pair (u,v) contributes to C[s] as h_v(1-h_u) and to C[-s]? If consider source v, target u with shift -s: term h_u(1-h_v). High if u high, v low. So if h_u>h_v, choose donor u, receiver v to reduce C[-s]. Thus for active absolute lag pair |s|, choose the donor as the higher of the two positions to reduce the high directed count among the two. Since max may be one direction, equalizing helps both. Good. So active move: choose active lag s, random valid pair (u source, v target). If h[v] >= h[u], donor=v, receiver=u; else donor=u, receiver=v. Step t to equalize maybe.
- Step t not full equalization because affects many lags; use small fraction of difference: t = min(diff, base * random?) If donor high, receiver low, moving some mass equalizes. t candidates = [min(diff * alpha, base)]? For continuous descent, maybe t = 0.25*diff or adaptive step. Implementation details top-level (no closures/lambdas):
Globals for optimization functions:
- `_opt_n`, `_opt_K`
Functions:
```
def _sum_con(x): return np.sum(x) - _opt_n/2.0
def _sum_con_jac(x): return np.ones(_opt_n)
```
But SLSQP constraint dict includes 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac. Need _opt_n set before. `_sum_con` uses float.

`_corr_full(x)` choose direct or FFT. For SLSQP many evals, use direct for n<=1024? Direct `np.correlate` is C but O(n^2); for n=512 ~262k operations, fast. FFT complex might similar. Use direct for simplicity and exactness.
```
def corr_full(x): return np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
```
For n large >1000, direct maybe slow; we can limit n ≤512. We'll choose n ≤384 maybe. Good.

`_topk_index(C, K)`: if K>=len return all; else if K==1 use argmax; else argpartition.

`_obj_topk(x)`:
```
C=corr_full(x)
K=_opt_K
if K==1: return C.max()
idx = np.argpartition(C, len(C)-K)[-K:]
return C[idx].mean()
```
If ties, fine. For K>1, SLSQP objective may not be maximum; but final check.
`_jac_topk(x)`:
```
n=_opt_n; C=corr_full(x); K=_opt_K
if K==1:
  s=int(np.argmax(C))-(n-1); return grad_corr_lag(s,x)
idx=argpartition...
grad=zeros(n)
for val in idx:
  s=int(val)-(n-1); grad += grad_corr_lag(s,x)
grad /= K
return grad
```
Potential for K=1 SLSQP with non-smooth; but we can use K=2 or 1 at end. For smoothness, use K=5,10,30,60. Then use coordinate.

Pseudo:
```
def coordinate_optimize(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    C = corr_full(h)
    cur = C.max()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_C = C.copy(); best_cur=cur
    rng = RandomState(seed)
    base = 0.05
    min_base=1e-4
    no_accept=0; accept_count=0
    period=1000
    top_k_active=30
    margin=1e-12
    while time.time()-t_start < time_limit:
       # update active lags occasionally
       active_idx = top K (maybe K=30) of C
       # choose move
       if rng.rand() < 0.7:
          # active: choose lag with probability maybe all active
          idx = active_idx[rng.randint(len(active_idx))]
          s = int(idx)-(n-1)
          if s>=0:
             j = rng.randint(0, n-s)
             u=j; v=j+s
          else:
             d=-s
             j = rng.randint(d, n)
             u=j+s; v=j  # source? Let's keep source j, target j+s? C[s] term h[j+s](1-h[j]) = target v, source u? For move direction, pair positions (src=j, tgt=j+s). tgt = j+s, src=j. If s<0, j from -s..n-1, tgt=j+s.
          # pair = (src, tgt)
          a = h[src]; b = h[tgt]
          if b >= a: donor=tgt; recv=src
          else: donor=src; recv=tgt
          # skip if donor==recv
          # step size: fraction of diff
          diff = abs(b-a)
          step = min(base, 0.25*diff) if diff>0 else base*0.1
          # Also maybe step=base if diff=0? no move.
       else:
          # random donor high, receiver low
          # choose donor p where h>eps; receiver q where h<1-eps
          # Could sample candidates until bounds
          p = rng.randint(n); q = rng.randint(n)
          while p==q: ...
          # ensure p can decrease, q can increase: if h[p] <= eps or h[q] >=1-eps, choose random from feasible arrays
          # Simpler: sample p from np.nonzero(h>eps), q from nonempty(1-h>eps)
          donor=p; recv=q
          # if donor low/recv high? direction to equalize: if h[donor] <= h[recv], swap roles? For random, choose donor higher value to lower peaks maybe.
          if h[donor] < h[recv]: swap
          diff=h[donor]-h[recv]
          step=min(base, 0.25*diff) if diff>eps else base/2
       if step <= 1e-8: step=base/2? maybe
       # Try candidate steps: step, step/2, step/4 maybe but ensure feasible: step <= min(h[donor],1-h[recv])
       step = min(step, h[donor], 1-h[recv])
       # We can also try push negative? donor decreases, receiver increases by construction.
       best_trial=None; best_newmax=None
       for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
          t=step*scale
          if t<=1e-10: continue
          delta=corr_delta(h, donor, recv, t)
          # To avoid full allocation? C_trial = C + delta; okay.
          new_C = C + delta
          new_max = new_C.max()
          if new_max < cur - 1e-12:
             # if not found or lower, store? copying new_C costly. Instead if accept immediately if first? To choose best we can store t and maybe delta? memory small.
             if best_newmax is None or new_max < best_newmax:
                best_newmax = new_max; best_t=t; best_delta=delta
       if best_newmax is not None:
          h[donor]-=best_t; h[recv]+=best_t
          C += best_delta
          cur = best_newmax
          # update best if cur*2/n < best_cur*2/n
          if cur < best_cur - 1e-12:
             best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
          accept_count +=1; no_accept=0
          # decay step slightly
          base = max(min_base, base*0.999)
       else:
          no_accept+=1
          if no_accept > 20:
             base *= 0.8
             if base < min_base:
                # random perturbation: add small noise and project maybe accept if not worse than best? Use restart from best with noise
                h = best_h.copy() + 0.02*rng.randn(n)
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                C = corr_full(h)
                cur = C.max()
                if cur > best_cur: # keep best
                   h = best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); cur=best_cur
                base = min(0.02, max(min_base, base*10)) # reheat small
                no_accept=0
       # If no improvement over 2000 steps, restart perturbed best
       if accept_count>0 and (iter_count % 5000 ==0?) ...
```

```
max_cand = 8
base_decay=0.999 per accept, 0.9 per 10 no_accept.
for each macro:
  cand_list=[]

  for _ in active_count:

    s = idx-n+1
    valid range...
    j = rng.randint(low, high)

    max_t = min(h[tgt], 1-h[src])
    if max_t > 1e-8: cand.append((tgt,src,max_t, 'active'))
  # random candidates
  for _ in random_count:
    p = rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
    if p==q continue
    if h[p] > h[q]: donor=p; recv=q; max_t=min(h[p],1-h[q])
    else: donor=q; recv=p; max_t=...
    if max_t>1e-8: cand.append
  # if no cand, reduce base or perturb
  best_newmax=inf

    # choose t base

  if found apply, else ...

Now, detail `coordinate_optimize` with top-level no closures. It can use a scratch `best_h` copy. Let's write:
```
def coordinate_optimize(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    t_start = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h,n).astype(float)
    C = corr_full(h)
    cur = float(C.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_C = C.copy(); best_cur = cur
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    base = 0.05
    min_base = 1e-5
    active_k = min(40, 2*n-1)
    active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
    no_accept=0
    macro=0
    recompute_counter=0
    while time.time()-t_start < time_limit:
        C = C  # current
        # recompute active_idx every 50 or if no_accept>20
        if macro % 50 == 0 or no_accept > 20:
             active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
        cand_list=[]
        n_cand=8
        n_active=max(1, int(n_cand*0.6))
        for _ in range(n_active):
             idx = active_idx[rng.randint(active_k)]
             s = int(idx) - (n-1)
             if s >= 0:
                 if n - 1 - s <= 0: continue
                 j = rng.randint(0, n-s)
             else:
                 d = -s
                 if d >= n: continue
                 j = rng.randint(d, n)
             tgt = j + s
             src = j
             max_t = min(h[tgt], 1.0 - h[src])
             if max_t > 1e-8:
                 # t_opt for reducing C_s
                 t_opt = 0.5*(1.0 - h[src] + h[tgt])
                 cand_list.append((tgt, src, max_t, t_opt))
        n_rand = n_cand - len(cand_list)
        for _ in range(max(0,n_rand)):
             p = rng.randint(n); q = rng.randint(n)
             if p == q: continue
             if h[p] > h[q]:
                 donor=p; recv=q
             else:
                 donor=q; recv=p
             max_t = min(h[donor], 1.0 - h[recv])
             if max_t > 1e-8:
                 # t_opt based on equalizing? use min(max_t, 0.5*(h[donor]-h[recv]) + ?)
                 diff = h[donor] - h[recv]
                 if diff > 0:
                    t_opt = 0.5*diff
                 else:
                    t_opt = 0.25*max_t
                 cand_list.append((donor, recv, max_t, t_opt))
        if not cand_list:
             no_accept += 5
             base *= 0.7
             if base < min_base:
                 # restart with small noise from best
                 h = best_h + 0.01*rng.randn(n)
                 h = project_feasible(h,n)
                 C = corr_full(h)
                 cur = float(C.max())
                 if cur > best_cur:
                     h = best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); cur=best_cur
                 base = max(min_base, 0.01)
             macro += 1
             continue
        best_new = cur; best_delta=None; best_t=0.0; best_p = -1
        # evaluate candidates
        eval_t_list = ???
        for donor, recv, max_t, t_opt in cand_list:
             # feasible t values
             # 1. base capped
             t1 = min(base, max_t)
             # 2. fraction of max_t
             t2 = min(0.25*max_t, max_t)
             # 3. t_opt capped
             t3 = min(max(max_t, 1e-9), max_t)???
             # create set without duplicates
             trial_ts = [t1]
             if t2 > 1e-8 and t2 != t1: trial_ts.append(t2)
             if t_opt > 1e-8 and abs(t_opt - t1)>1e-8 and abs(t_opt - t2)>1e-8: trial_ts.append(t_opt)
             # Also maybe full max_t if binary-ish
             if max_t > 0.99 and rng.rand() < 0.3: trial_ts.append(max_t)
             for t in trial_ts:
                 if t <= 1e-10 or t > max_t + 1e-12: continue
                 delta = correlation_delta(h, donor, recv, float(t))
                 # Instead of full C+delta, compute max efficiently? Use np.add(C, delta, out=trial)? Need scratch.
                 trial = C + delta
                 vm = float(trial.max())
                 if vm < best_new:
                     best_new = vm; best_delta = delta; best_t = t; best_p = donor; best_q=recv
        if best_delta is not None:
             h[best_p] -= best_t
             h[best_q] += best_t
             # update C in-place
             C += best_delta
             cur = float(best_new)
             # adjust sum? sum exact? floating.
             if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
                 best_cur = cur; best_h = h.copy(); best_C = C.copy()
             no_accept = 0
             base = max(min_base, base * 0.999)
        else:
             no_accept += 1
             base = max(min_base, base * 0.9)
             if no_accept > 200:
                 # perturb from best
                 h = best_h + 0.02*rng.randn(n)
                 h = project_feasible(h,n)
                 C = corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
                 if cur > best_cur:
                    h=best_h.copy(); C=best_C.copy(); cur=best_cur
                 base = max(0.01, min_base)
                 no_accept = 0
        macro += 1
        recompute_counter += 1
        if recompute_counter >= 2000:
             recompute_counter = 0
             h = project_feasible(h,n)
             C = corr_full(h)
             cur = float(C.max())
             if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
                 best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
             # if C drift worse than cur? keep h
    return (best_h, best_cur * (2.0 / n))
```

```
n_active_candidates=6
# pick top active lags by C (already active_idx)
lag_list = active_idx[np.argsort(C[active_idx])[-L:]]  # descending
for idx in lag_list:
   s = idx-(n-1)
   if s>=0:
      src_idx=np.arange(0,n-s); tgt_idx=src_idx+s; terms=h[tgt_idx]*(1-h[src_idx])
   else:
      d=-s; src_idx=np.arange(d,n); tgt_idx=src_idx-d; terms=...
   valid = (h[tgt_idx]>1e-8) & (h[src_idx]<1-1e-8)
   if any:
      # choose random among top max(2, 10% of valid) by terms
      tvals=terms.copy(); tvals[~valid]=-1
      kpick=min(3, int(len(tvals)*0.1)+1)
      if kpick >0: picks=np.argpartition(tvals,-kpick)[-kpick:]
      for pos in picks: if tvals[pos]>1e-8: add candidate (tgt,pos, max_t, t_opt)
```

def rounded_candidates(h,n):
   cands=[]

      hb=(h>0.5).astype(float)

   # near binary: snap <0.05=0, >0.95=1, keep middle? But sum adjust? Snap then project (clip and sum) may produce fractional centered.

`optimize_at_n` top-level:
```
def optimize_at_n(n, starts, time_limit, seed, global_best_threshold=inf):
   t_start=time.time()
   local_best_h=starts[0]; local_best_c=compute_c5(local_best_h)
   # SLSQP on starts? maybe first 2
   for i,h0 in enumerate(starts[:min(2,len(starts))]):
       tl = time_limit - (time.time()-t_start)
       if tl < 30: break
       # time split: for first 0.4, second 0.2 of limit
       stage_time = min(120, tl* (0.5 if i==0 else 0.3))
       h1 = slsqp_polish(h0, n, stage_time, seed+100*i)
       c1=compute_c5(h1); if c1<local_best_c: update
   # coordinate on best and starts
   for i,h0 in enumerate([local_best_h] + [s for s in starts if s is not local_best_h][:2]):
       tl=...
       if tl<30: break
       # coordinate time: first 0.4 limit, others 0.15
       coord_time = min(300, tl* (0.5 if i==0 else 0.25))
       h2,c2=coordinate_optimize(h0,n,coord_time,seed+200*i)
       if c2<local_best_c: update local_best_h=c2
       # immediate SLSQP polish small
       tl=...
       if tl>20:
          h3=slsqp_polish(local_best_h,n,min(60,tl*0.3),seed+300)
          c3=compute_c5(h3); update
       # coordinate final small
       if tl>20:
          h4,c4=coordinate_optimize(local_best_h,n,min(80,tl*0.4),seed+400)
          update
   return local_best_h, local_best_c
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.array(h,float).copy()
   if h.size!=n: ...
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2.0
   for _ in range(100):
      diff=target-np.sum(h)
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      # adjust only free variables to preserve bounds
      if diff>0:
         mask=h<1.0
         if not mask.any(): h+=diff/len? then clip
         h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
      else:
         mask=h>0.0
         if not mask.any(): ...
         h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # final adjust largest free
   diff=target-np.sum(h)
   if abs(diff)>0:
      if diff>0:
         idx=np.argmax(1.0-h); h[idx]+=diff
      else:
         idx=np.argmax(h); h[idx]+=diff
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
```
This projects exactly but may alter if at bounds. Good.

D: Dpoints: mid 2t^2; diff -t^2. Add -D: mid -2t^2; diff +t^2. Good.

def make_starts(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng):
   starts=[]
   if parent_h is not None and parent_n>0:
       xp=(np.arange(parent_n)+0.5)*2.0/parent_n
       xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
       h0=np.interp(xn, xp, parent_h, left=parent_h[0], right=parent_h[-1])
       h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
       starts.append(h0)
       hsym=0.5*(h0 + h0[::-1])
       starts.append(project_feasible(hsym,n))
       for sc in (0.03,0.08):
           starts.append(project_feasible(h0 + sc*rng.randn(n), n))
       if n % 2 ==0:
           hb=(h0>=0.5).astype(float)
           hb=binary_adjust(hb,n)
           starts.append(hb)
       # random binary
       hb2=np.zeros(n); if n%2==0: hb2[rng.permutation(n)[:n//2]]=1
       starts.append(hb2)
   else:
       # defaults
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
       c=0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0))
       starts.append(project_feasible(c,n))
       c2=0.5 + 0.4*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0)) + 0.1*np.cos(3*np.pi*(x-1.0))
       starts.append(project_feasible(c2,n))
       for sc in (0.05,0.1): starts.append(project_feasible(c+sc*rng.randn(n),n))
       if n%2==0:
          hb=np.zeros(n); hb[rng.permutation(n)[:n//2]]=1; starts.append(hb)
   # duplicates? not needed. Limit to 4? To save time. Return starts[:4]
```

def rounded_binary(h,n):
   if n%2 !=0: return project_feasible(h,n)
   m=n//2
   hb=(h>=0.5).astype(float)
   ones=np.where(hb==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(hb==0)[0]
   if len(ones)>m:
       order=np.argsort(h[ones])[len(ones)-m:m]? Need remove len(ones)-m smallest h among ones: idx = ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:len(ones)-m]]; hb[idx]=0
   elif len(ones)<m:
       idx = zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])[:m-len(ones)]]; hb[idx]=1
   return hb

`slsqp_polish` code:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    t0=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_con_jac}]
    _g_n=n
    stages=[(30,80),(20,120),(10,160),(5,200),(2,250),(1,300)]
    for K,maxit in stages:
       if time.time()-t0 >= time_limit or time.time()-t0/time? Actually check
       if remaining < 4: break
       _g_K=K
       maxit_stage = maxit
       # reduce if little time: maxiter maybe min(maxit, int(remaining*20)+1)
       try:
          res=minimize(_obj_topk,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,jac=_jac_topk,options={'maxiter':maxit_stage,'ftol':1e-15,'iprint':0})
          if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       except Exception:
          pass
    return h
```

    if max_t > 1e-8:
       t_opt=0.5*(1.0 - h[src_i] + h[tgt_i])
       add candidate if not duplicate maybe.
```

Pseudo coordinate with top scan:
```
def coordinate_optimize(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    if time_limit <= 0: return h, compute_c5(h)
    t_start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    C=corr_full(h); cur=...
    best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
    rng=...
    base=0.04; min_base=1e-5
    active_k = min(30, 2*n-1)
    active_idx = top
    no_accept=0; macro=0; recompute=0; reheat_count=0
    while True:
       if time.time()-t_start > time_limit-1: break
       rev_one=(1-h)[::-1]
       if macro % 50 ==0 or no_accept>20:
          active_idx=...
       cand=[]
       # top scan
       if active_k>0:
          lag_order = np.argsort(C[active_idx])[::-1]
          top_lags = active_idx[lag_order[:5]]
          for idx in top_lags:
             if len(cand)>=8: break
             s=int(idx)-(n-1)
             ... add up to 2 cand
       # random active
       for _ in range(3):
          if len(cand)>=MAX_CANDS: break
          idx=active_idx[rng.randint(active_k)]
          s=...
          random j...
          add
       # random global
       for _ in range(3):
          if len(cand)>=MAX_CANDS: break
          p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)...
       if not cand:
          # reheat
          ...
          macro+=1; continue
       # evaluate candidates
       best_new=cur; best_delta=None; ...
       for donor,recv,max_t,t_opt in cand:
          # t list
          t1 = min(base, max_t)
          t2 = 0.25*max_t
          t3 = min(max_t, max(0.0, t_opt))
          # choose unique using small tolerance
          # also if max_t > 0.9: t4=max_t
          for t in ...:
             if t <= 1e-9: continue
             if t > max_t: t=max_t
             delta=correlation_delta(h, donor, recv, t, rev_one)
             # Use maybe only if candidate could affect max? We can quick bound? no.
             vm = float((C + delta).max())
             if vm < best_new - 1e-15:
                best_new=vm; best_delta=delta; best_t=t; best_donor=donor; best_recv=recv
       if best_delta is not None:
          h[best_donor] -= best_t; h[best_recv] += best_t
          C += best_delta
          cur=best_new
          if cur < best_cur - 1e-15:
             best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
          no_accept=0
          base=max(min_base, base*0.999)
       else:
          no_accept+=1
          if no_accept % 10 ==0: base=max(min_base, base*0.85)
          if no_accept > 250 and reheat_count < 30:
             scale = 0.01 + 0.03*rng.rand()
             h = project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
             C = corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
             # don't revert; this is current start
             base = 0.02
             no_accept=0
             reheat_count += 1
             active_idx=...
       macro+=1
       recompute += 1
       if recompute >= 1000:
          recompute=0
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          C=corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
          if cur < best_cur - 1e-15:
             best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
          active_idx=...
    # final recompute best_h
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    # if final_c < best_cur*2/n? return min but best_h exact.
    return (best_h, final_c)
```

def optimize_at_n(n, starts, time_limit, seed):
   t_start=time.time()
   if not starts: return None, inf
   h_best=starts[0].copy(); c_best=compute_c5(h_best)
   # SLSQP first starts
   n_slsqp = min(2, len(starts))
   slq_times = [0.35, 0.25]
   for i in range(n_slsqp):
      rem = time_limit - (time.time()-t_start)
      if rem < 20: break
      t_s = min(rem*slq_times[i], 120.0)
      if t_s < 5: continue
      h1=slsqp_polish(starts[i], n, t_s, seed+10*i)
      c1=compute_c5(h1)
      if c1 < c_best: c_best=c_best? update; h_best=h1.copy()
   # coordinate starts: best, starts[0] if not best, starts[1]
   coord_starts=[]
   coord_starts.append(h_best)
   if len(starts)>0 and not (len(starts[0])==len(h_best) and np.allclose(starts[0],h_best,rtol=1e-6,atol=1e-6)):
       coord_starts.append(starts[0])
   if len(starts)>1 and ...: coord_starts.append(starts[1])
   coord_fracs=[0.6,0.2,0.2]
   for j,h0 in enumerate(coord_starts):
      rem = time_limit - (time.time()-t_start)
      if rem < 30: break
      t_c = rem * coord_fracs[j] if j==0 else min(80.0, rem*0.2)
      if t_c < 20: continue
      h2,c2=coordinate_optimize(h0, n, t_c, seed+200+j)
      if c2 < c_best: c_best=c2; h_best=h2.copy()
      # SLSQP small polish after first coordinate
      rem = time_limit - (time.time()-t_start)
      if rem > 30 and j==0:
         h3=slsqp_polish(h_best,n,min(60.0, rem*0.25), seed+500)
         c3=compute_c5(h3)
         if c3 < c_best: update
         # coordinate small final
         rem=...
         if rem>30:
            h4,c4=coordinate_optimize(h_best,n,min(60.0,rem*0.4),seed+600)
            if c4<c_best: update
   return h_best, compute_c5(h_best)
```

`threshold_binary` top-level:
```
def threshold_binary(h,n,th):
    if n % 2 != 0:
       return project_feasible(h,n)
    m=n//2
    hb=(h>=th).astype(float)
    ones=np.where(hb==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(hb==0)[0]
    if len(ones)>m:
       idx=ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:len(ones)-m]]
       hb[idx]=0.0
    elif len(ones)<m:
       idx=zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])[:m-len(ones)]]
       hb[idx]=1.0
    return hb
```
For th=0.5, same as rounded_binary. Use in starts.

Let's write code now mentally. Need avoid syntax errors.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_g_n = 0
_g_K = 1

def project_feasible(h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
        # resample? should not happen
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.5)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - np.sum(h)
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0.0:
                h += diff / n
            else:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0.0:
                h += diff / n
            else:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            add = max(diff, -h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
            # distribute tiny residual over free variables
            if diff > 0:
                mask = h < 1.0
                if mask.any(): h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
            else:
                mask = h > 0.0
                if mask.any(): h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
            h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
If residual can't fit? diff small. Good.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n<=0: return float('inf')
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(c.max() * (2.0/n))
```

`corr_full`: same but no scale.

`grad_corr_lag` slices: Need convert h float.
```
def grad_corr_lag(s, h):
    n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n,float)
    if s >=0:
       if s ==0:
          g += (1.0 - h)
          g -= h
       else:
          g[s:] += (1.0 - h[:n-s])
          g[:n-s] -= h[s:]
    else:
       d = -s
       if d ==0: ...
       g[:n-d] += (1.0 - h[d:])
       g[d:] -= h[:n-d]
    return g
```
For s=0, target source coincide; formula g += (1-h) and g -= h => 1-2h. Good. In s>=0 else with s=0, g[0:]+=1-h; g[:n]-=h. OK.

`_obj_topk`:
```
def _obj_topk(x):
    n=_g_n
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100
    C = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')
    K = int(_g_K)
    if K <=1: return float(C.max())
    if K >= C.size: return float(C.mean())
    idx = np.argpartition(C, C.size-K)[C.size-K:]
    return float(np.mean(C[idx]))
```
`_jac_topk` similar. For K>=C.size, gradient mean over all active? If all active, could loop all 2n-1 maybe O(n^2). But not in stages unless n small? Could happen if C.size=31 and K=40. Not likely n≥96. If happens, return subgradient of mean? We can loop all indices; n small. Implement:
```
if K>=C.size:
   idx=np.arange(C.size)
   count=C.size
else: idx=...
   count=K
grad=zeros
for val in idx: s=int(val)-(n-1); grad+=grad_corr_lag(s,x)
grad /= float(count)
return grad
```
For K=1, loop one.

`make_trial_ts` top-level:
```
def make_trial_ts(base, max_t, t_opt, include_full):
   if max_t <= 1e-9: return []
   ts=[]
   vals = [base, 0.1*max_t, 0.25*max_t, t_opt]
   if include_full and max_t > 0.9: vals.append(max_t)
   for tv in vals:
       try: t=float(tv)
       except: continue
       if t <= 1e-9: continue
       if t > max_t: t = max_t
       if t <= 1e-9: continue
       ok=True
       for u in ts:
          if abs(u-t) <= 1e-7: ok=False; break
       if ok: ts.append(t)
   return ts
```

`correlation_delta`:
```
def correlation_delta(h, p, q, t, rev_one=None):
    n=len(h); L=2*n-1
    if rev_one is None: rev_one=(1.0-h)[::-1]
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    # A
    delta[p:p+n] += (-t) * rev_one
    delta[q:q+n] += (t) * rev_one
    # B
    sp = n - 1 - p
    delta[sp:sp+n] += (t) * h
    sq = n - 1 - q
    delta[sq:sq+n] += (-t) * h
    tt = t*t
    delta[n-1] -= 2.0*tt
    idx1 = n - 1 + (q - p)
    delta[idx1] += tt
    idx2 = n - 1 - (q - p)
    delta[idx2] += tt
    return delta
```
Need ensure p,q ints. If p/q are numpy ints, slicing okay. `idx1` int.

Now, `slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    t0=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    if time_limit <=0: return h
    _g_n=n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_con_jac}]
    stages=[(30,40),(20,50),(10,70),(5,90),(2,120),(1,160)]
    for K, mi in stages:
       if time.time()-t0 >= time_limit - 2: break
       _g_K=K
       # maybe scale maxiter by remaining
       mi2 = mi
       try:
          res = minimize(_obj_topk, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=_jac_topk, options={'maxiter':mi2, 'ftol':1e-15, 'iprint':0})
          if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
       except Exception:
          pass
    return h
```
Note global `_g_K` set but not declared? In function, assigning `_g_K` makes local unless global. Need `global _g_n, _g_K`. Add at top. Same for constraints? `_sum_con` uses global.

`_sum_con`:
```
def _sum_con(x): return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*_g_n)
def _sum_con_jac(x): return np.ones(_g_n, dtype=float)
```

Now, `coordinate_optimize` code. Need top-level no nested. Let's write with careful variables.
```
def coordinate_optimize(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    if time_limit <= 0:
       return (h, compute_c5(h))
    t_start=time.time()
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    C=corr_full(h)
    cur=float(C.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_cur=cur
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    base=0.04; min_base=1e-5
    L=C.size
    active_k=min(30,L)
    if active_k>0:
       if active_k>=L: active_idx=np.arange(L)
       else: active_idx=np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
    else: active_idx=np.empty(0)
    no_accept=0; macro=0; recompute=0; reheat=0
    max_cands=12
    while time.time() - t_start < time_limit - 1.0:
       if active_idx.size == 0:
           active_idx = np.arange(L)
           active_k = L
       rev_one=(1.0-h)[::-1]
       if macro % 50 == 0 or no_accept > 20:
           if active_k >= L: active_idx = np.arange(L)
           else: active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
       cand=[]
       # top scan if due
       if (macro % 25 == 0 or no_accept > 8) and active_idx.size > 0:
           order = np.argsort(C[active_idx])[::-1]
           top_lags = active_idx[order[:4]]
           for idx in top_lags:
              if len(cand) >= 8: break
              s=int(idx) - (n-1)
              if s >= 0:
                 m = n - s
                 if m <= 0: continue
                 src = np.arange(0, m)
                 tgt = src + s
              else:
                 d = -s; m = n - d
                 if m <= 0: continue
                 src = np.arange(d, n)
                 tgt = src - d
              terms = h[tgt] * (1.0 - h[src])
              valid = (h[tgt] > 1e-8) & (h[src] < 1.0 - 1e-8)
              if not np.any(valid): continue
              scores = np.where(valid, terms, -1.0)
              nv = int(np.count_nonzero(valid))
              kpick = 2 if nv < 4 else 3
              kpick = min(kpick, nv)
              if kpick <=0: continue
              picks = np.argpartition(scores, -kpick)[-kpick:]
              picks = picks[scores[picks] > 1e-8]
              for pos in picks:
                  if len(cand) >= max_cands: break
                  si=int(src[pos]); ti=int(tgt[pos])
                  mt = min(float(h[ti]), float(1.0 - h[si]))
                  if mt <= 1e-8: continue
                  topt = 0.5 * (1.0 - float(h[si]) + float(h[ti]))
                  cand.append((ti, si, mt, topt))
       # random active
       n_rand_active=4
       for _ in range(n_rand_active):
          if active_idx.size == 0: break
          if len(cand) >= max_cands: break
          idx = int(active_idx[rng.randint(active_idx.size)])
          s=idx-(n-1)
          if s >=0:
             m=n-s
             if m <=0: continue
             j=int(rng.randint(0,m))
          else:
             d=-s; m=n-d
             if m <=0: continue
             j=int(rng.randint(d,n))
          ti=j+s; si=j
          mt=min(float(h[ti]), float(1.0-h[si]))
          if mt > 1e-8:
             topt=0.5*(1.0-float(h[si])+float(h[ti]))
             cand.append((ti,si,mt,topt))
       # random global
       n_rand_global=3
       for _ in range(n_rand_global):
          if len(cand) >= max_cands: break
          p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
          if p == q: continue
          if h[p] > h[q]:
             donor=p; recv=q
          else:
             donor=q; recv=p
          mt=min(float(h[donor]), float(1.0-h[recv]))
          if mt > 1e-8:
             diff=float(h[donor])-float(h[recv])
             topt=0.5*diff if diff > 0 else 0.25*mt
             cand.append((donor,recv,mt,topt))
       if not cand:
          no_accept += 5
          base = max(min_base, base*0.7)
          if base < min_base or no_accept > 300:
             scale = 0.01 + 0.03*float(rng.rand())
             h = project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
             C = corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
             base=0.02; no_accept=0; reheat+=1
             if active_k >= C.size: active_idx=np.arange(L)
             else: active_idx=np.argpartition(C,-active_k)[-active_k:]
          macro += 1
          continue
       best_new=cur; best_delta=None; best_t=0.0; best_d=-1; best_r=-1
       include_full = False
       # Possibly enable full swaps if h is near binary? Detect tolerance maybe sum abs(h-0.5)>0.2*n
       binaryish = float(np.mean(np.abs(h - 0.5))) > 0.25
       for donor, recv, mt, topt in cand:
          tlist = make_trial_ts(base, mt, topt, binaryish)
          for t in tlist:
             delta = correlation_delta(h, donor, recv, t, rev_one)
             # quick max
             vm = float(np.max(C + delta))
             if vm < best_new - 1e-14:
                best_new = vm; best_delta = delta; best_t = t; best_d = donor; best_r = recv
       if best_delta is not None:
          h[best_d] -= best_t
          h[best_r] += best_t
          C += best_delta
          cur = best_new
          if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
             best_cur = cur; best_h = h.copy()
          no_accept = 0
          base = max(min_base, base*0.999)
       else:
          no_accept += 1
          if no_accept % 8 == 0:
             base = max(min_base, base*0.85)
          if no_accept > 300:
             scale = 0.01 + 0.03*float(rng.rand())
             h = project_feasible(best_h + scale*rng.randn(n), n)
             C = corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
             base = 0.02; no_accept=0; reheat += 1
             if active_k >= C.size: active_idx=np.arange(L)
             else: active_idx=np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
       macro += 1
       recompute += 1
       if recompute >= 800:
          recompute=0
          h = project_feasible(h,n)
          C = corr_full(h); cur=float(C.max())
          if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
             best_cur=cur; best_h=h.copy()
          if active_k >= C.size: active_idx=np.arange(L)
          else: active_idx=np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    # if final_c is slightly worse than best_cur*2/n due projection? best_h copied; use min
    if final_c > best_cur * (2.0/n) and best_cur * (2.0/n) < float('inf'):
         # Could recompute C of best_h to get true count
         pass
    return (best_h, final_c)
```

```
def make_starts(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng):
   starts=[]
   if parent_h is not None:
       pn=parent_h.size
       if pn >0:
          xp=(np.arange(pn)+0.5)*2.0/pn
          xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
          h0=np.interp(xn,xp,parent_h,left=parent_h[0],right=parent_h[-1])
          h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
          starts.append(h0)
          hsym=0.5*(h0 + h0[::-1])
          starts.append(project_feasible(hsym,n))
          if n %2 ==0:
             hb=rounded_binary(h0,n)
             if hb is not None and hb.size==n: starts.append(hb)
          starts.append(project_feasible(h0 + 0.04*rng.randn(n), n))
          starts.append(project_feasible(h0 + 0.09*rng.randn(n), n))
   else:
       x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
       c=0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0))
       starts.append(project_feasible(c,n))
       c2=0.5 + 0.42*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0)) + 0.08*np.cos(3.0*np.pi*(x-1.0))
       starts.append(project_feasible(c2,n))
       starts.append(project_feasible(c + 0.05*rng.randn(n),n))
       if n %2 ==0:
          hb=np.zeros(n,float)
          hb[np.random.RandomState(int(rng.randint(1e9))).permutation(n)[:n//2]]=1.0
          starts.append(hb)
   # remove duplicates? not necessary; limit 5
   out=[]
   for h in starts:
       out.append(h)
       if len(out)>=5: break
   return out
```
In no parent random binary uses nested RandomState; top-level okay but ugly. Use rng.permutation.
Need `rounded_binary` defined before.

`rounded_binary`:
```
def rounded_binary(h,n):
   if n % 2 !=0:
      return project_feasible(h,n)
   h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
   m=n//2
   hb=(h >= 0.5).astype(float)
   ones=np.where(hb==1.0)[0]
   zeros=np.where(hb==0.0)[0]
   if ones.size > m:
      idx=ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:ones.size-m]]
      hb[idx]=0.0
   elif ones.size < m:
      idx=zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])[:m-ones.size]]
      hb[idx]=1.0
   return hb
```
For threshold variants final use `threshold_binary`.

`threshold_binary` similar.

`optimize_at_n`:
```
def optimize_at_n(n, starts, time_limit, seed):
    t_start=time.time()
    if not starts: return None, inf
    h_best=starts[0].copy()
    c_best=compute_c5(h_best)
    # SLSQP
    slq_f = [0.25,0.15]
    slq_max = [120.0,60.0]
    for i in range(min(2,len(starts))):
        elapsed=time.time()-t_start
        if elapsed + 20 >= time_limit: break
        t_s=min((time_limit-elapsed)*slq_f[i], slq_max[i])
        if t_s < 5: continue
        h1=slsqp_polish(starts[i], n, t_s, seed+100*i)
        c1=compute_c5(h1)
        if c1 < c_best:
            c_best=c1; h_best=h1.copy()
    # coordinate best
    elapsed=time.time()-t_start
    if time_limit - elapsed > 30:
       t_c = min((time_limit-elapsed)*0.55, 450.0)
       if t_c < 20: t_c = time_limit - elapsed - 5
       h2,c2=coordinate_optimize(h_best,n,t_c,seed+2000)
       if c2 < c_best: c_best=c2; h_best=h2.copy()
       elapsed=time.time()-t_start
       if time_limit - elapsed > 20:
          h3=slsqp_polish(h_best,n,min(60.0,(time_limit-elapsed)*0.25),seed+3000)
          c3=compute_c5(h3)
          if c3 < c_best: c_best=c3; h_best=h3.copy()
          elapsed=time.time()-t_start
          if time_limit - elapsed > 20:
             h4,c4=coordinate_optimize(h_best,n,min(80.0,(time_limit-elapsed)*0.4),seed+4000)
             if c4 < c_best: c_best=c4; h_best=h4.copy()
    # coordinate other starts
    s_idx=[]
    if len(starts)>1: s_idx.append(1)
    if len(starts)>2: s_idx.append(2)
    for it,s in enumerate(s_idx):
       elapsed=time.time()-t_start
       if time_limit - elapsed < 30: break
       frac = 0.25 if it==0 else 0.15
       t_c=min((time_limit-elapsed)*frac, 180.0)
       if t_c < 15: continue
       h2,c2=coordinate_optimize(starts[s],n,t_c,seed+5000+it)
       if c2 < c_best: c_best=c2; h_best=h2.copy()
       # maybe small slsqp if improved much? skip
    # final small slsqp if time
    elapsed=time.time()-t_start
    if time_limit - elapsed > 25:
       h3=slsqp_polish(h_best,n,min(50.0,(time_limit-elapsed)*0.6),seed+6000)
       c3=compute_c5(h3)
       if c3 < c_best: c_best=c3; h_best=h3.copy()
    final_c=compute_c5(h_best)
    return (h_best, final_c)
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_g_n = 0
_g_K = 1


def project_feasible(h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
            h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.5)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - np.sum(h)
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0.0:
                h += diff / n
            else:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0.0:
                h += diff / n
            else:
                h[mask] += diff / cnt
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            add = max(diff, -h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
            if diff > 0:
                mask = h < 1.0
                if mask.any():
                    h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
            else:
                mask = h > 0.0
                if mask.any():
                    h[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(c.max() * (2.0 / n))


def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def grad_corr_lag(s, h):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if s >= 0:
        g[s:] += (1.0 - h[:n - s])
        g[:n - s] -= h[s:]
    else:
        d = -s
        g[:n - d] += (1.0 - h[d:])
        g[d:] -= h[:n - d]
    return g


def _sum_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * _g_n)


def _sum_con_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_g_n, dtype=float)


def _obj_topk(x):
    n = _g_n
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100
    C = corr_full(x)
    K = int(_g_K)
    if K <= 1:
        return float(C.max())
    if K >= C.size:
        return float(C.mean())
    idx = np.argpartition(C, C.size - K)[C.size - K:]
    return float(np.mean(C[idx]))


def _jac_topk(x):
    n = _g_n
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    C = corr_full(x)
    K = int(_g_K)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if K <= 1:
        s = int(np.argmax(C)) - (n - 1)
        return grad_corr_lag(s, x)
    if K >= C.size:
        idx = np.arange(C.size)
        count = float(C.size)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(C, C.size - K)[C.size - K:]
        count = float(K)
    for val in idx:
        s = int(val) - (n - 1)
        grad += grad_corr_lag(s, x)
    grad /= count
    return grad


def make_trial_ts(base, max_t, t_opt, include_full):
    if max_t <= 1e-9:
        return []
    ts = []
    vals = [base, 0.1 * max_t, 0.25 * max_t, t_opt]
    if include_full and max_t > 0.9:
        vals.append(max_t)
    for tv in vals:
        try:
            t = float(tv)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if t <= 1e-9:
            continue
        if t > max_t:
            t = float(max_t)
        if t <= 1e-9:
            continue
        ok = True
        for u in ts:
            if abs(u - t) <= 1e-7:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            ts.append(t)
    return ts


def correlation_delta(h, p, q, t, rev_one=None):
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if rev_one is None:
        rev_one = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)

    # A: source-delta contribution
    delta[p:p + n] += (-t) * rev_one
    delta[q:q + n] += (t) * rev_one

    # B: subtract target/source h[j] * delta[j]
    sp = n - 1 - p
    delta[sp:sp + n] += (t) * h
    sq = n - 1 - q
    delta[sq:sq + n] += (-t) * h

    # D: subtract second-order product of delta entries
    tt = t * t
    delta[n - 1] -= 2.0 * tt
    idx1 = n - 1 + (q - p)
    delta[idx1] += tt
    idx2 = n - 1 - (q - p)
    delta[idx2] += tt

    return delta


def slsqp_polish(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    global _g_n, _g_K
    t0 = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return h

    _g_n = n
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac}]

    stages = [(30, 40), (20, 50), (10, 70), (5, 90), (2, 120), (1, 160)]
    for K, mi in stages:
        if time.time() - t0 >= time_limit - 2.0:
            break
        # Among the very last small stages, make sure we stop normally.
        if time.time() - t0 + 5.0 >= time_limit and K > 2:
            break
        _g_K = K
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _obj_topk, h,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                jac=_jac_topk,
                options={'maxiter': int(mi), 'ftol': 1e-15, 'iprint': 0},
            )
            if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return h


def coordinate_optimize(h, n, time_limit, seed):
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return h, compute_c5(h)

    t_start = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    C = corr_full(h)
    cur = float(C.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_cur = cur

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    base = 0.04
    min_base = 1e-5
    L = C.size
    active_k = min(30, L)
    if active_k >= L:
        active_idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]

    no_accept = 0
    macro = 0
    recompute_counter = 0
    reheat_count = 0
    max_cands = 12

    while time.time() - t_start < time_limit - 1.0:
        if active_idx.size == 0:
            active_idx = np.arange(L)
            active_k = L
        if active_k >= L:
            active_idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]

        rev_one = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
        cand = []

        # Directed scan of the worst terms from a few active lags.
        if (macro % 25 == 0 or no_accept > 8) and active_idx.size > 0:
            order = np.argsort(C[active_idx])[::-1]
            top_lags = active_idx[order[:4]]
            for idx in top_lags:
                if len(cand) >= 8:
                    break
                s = int(idx) - (n - 1)
                if s >= 0:
                    m = n - s
                    if m <= 0:
                        continue
                    src = np.arange(0, m)
                    tgt = src + s
                else:
                    d = -s
                    m = n - d
                    if m <= 0:
                        continue
                    src = np.arange(d, n)
                    tgt = src - d

                terms = h[tgt] * (1.0 - h[src])
                valid = (h[tgt] > 1e-8) & (h[src] < 1.0 - 1e-8)
                if not np.any(valid):
                    continue
                scores = np.where(valid, terms, -1.0)
                nv = int(np.count_nonzero(valid))
                kpick = 2 if nv < 4 else 3
                kpick = min(kpick, nv)
                if kpick <= 0:
                    continue
                picks = np.argpartition(scores, -kpick)[-kpick:]
                picks = picks[scores[picks] > 1e-8]
                for pos in picks:
                    if len(cand) >= max_cands:
                        break
                    si = int(src[pos])
                    ti = int(tgt[pos])
                    mt = min(float(h[ti]), float(1.0 - h[si]))
                    if mt <= 1e-8:
                        continue
                    topt = 0.5 * (1.0 - float(h[si]) + float(h[ti]))
                    cand.append((ti, si, mt, topt))

        # Random directed moves from active lags.
        for _ in range(4):
            if active_idx.size == 0 or len(cand) >= max_cands:
                break
            idx = int(active_idx[rng.randint(active_idx.size)])
            s = idx - (n - 1)
            if s >= 0:
                m = n - s
                if m <= 0:
                    continue
                j = int(rng.randint(0, m))
            else:
                d = -s
                m = n - d
                if m <= 0:
                    continue
                j = int(rng.randint(d, n))
            ti = j + s
            si = j
            mt = min(float(h[ti]), float(1.0 - h[si]))
            if mt > 1e-8:
                topt = 0.5 * (1.0 - float(h[si]) + float(h[ti]))
                cand.append((ti, si, mt, topt))

        # Random global high->low moves.
        for _ in range(3):
            if len(cand) >= max_cands:
                break
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if p == q:
                continue
            if h[p] > h[q]:
                donor = p
                recv = q
            else:
                donor = q
                recv = p
            mt = min(float(h[donor]), float(1.0 - h[recv]))
            if mt > 1e-8:
                diff = float(h[donor]) - float(h[recv])
                topt = 0.5 * diff if diff > 0.0 else 0.25 * mt
                cand.append((donor, recv, mt, topt))

        if not cand:
            no_accept += 5
            base = max(min_base, base * 0.7)
            if base < min_base or no_accept > 300:
                scale = 0.01 + 0.03 * float(rng.rand())
                h = project_feasible(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), n)
                C = corr_full(h)
                cur = float(C.max())
                base = 0.02
                no_accept = 0
                reheat_count += 1
                if active_k >= L:
                    active_idx = np.arange(L)
                else:
                    active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]
            macro += 1
            continue

        best_new = cur
        best_delta = None
        best_t = 0.0
        best_d = -1
        best_r = -1

        binaryish = float(np.mean(np.abs(h - 0.5))) > 0.25

        for donor, recv, mt, topt in cand:
            for t in make_trial_ts(base, mt, topt, binaryish):
                delta = correlation_delta(h, donor, recv, t, rev_one)
                vm = float(np.max(C + delta))
                if vm < best_new - 1e-14:
                    best_new = vm
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_t = t
                    best_d = donor
                    best_r = recv

        if best_delta is not None:
            h[best_d] -= best_t
            h[best_r] += best_t
            C += best_delta
            cur = best_new
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
                best_cur = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            no_accept = 0
            base = max(min_base, base * 0.999)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept % 8 == 0:
                base = max(min_base, base * 0.85)
            if no_accept > 300:
                scale = 0.01 + 0.03 * float(rng.rand())
                h = project_feasible(best_h + scale * rng.randn(n), n)
                C = corr_full(h)
                cur = float(C.max())
                base = 0.02
                no_accept = 0
                reheat_count += 1
                if active_k >= L:
                    active_idx = np.arange(L)
                else:
                    active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]

        macro += 1
        recompute_counter += 1
        if recompute_counter >= 800:
            recompute_counter = 0
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            C = corr_full(h)
            cur = float(C.max())
            if cur < best_cur - 1e-14:
                best_cur = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            if active_k >= L:
                active_idx = np.arange(L)
            else:
                active_idx = np.argpartition(C, -active_k)[-active_k:]

    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, final_c


def rounded_binary(h, n):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    m = n // 2
    hb = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    ones = np.where(hb == 1.0)[0]
    zeros = np.where(hb == 0.0)[0]
    if ones.size > m:
        idx = ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:ones.size - m]]
        hb[idx] = 0.0
    elif ones.size < m:
        idx = zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])[:m - ones.size]]
        hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb


def threshold_binary(h, n, th):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    m = n // 2
    hb = (h >= th).astype(float)
    ones = np.where(hb == 1.0)[0]
    zeros = np.where(hb == 0.0)[0]
    if ones.size > m:
        idx = ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:ones.size - m]]
        hb[idx] = 0.0
    elif ones.size < m:
        idx = zeros[np.argsort(-h[zeros])[:m - ones.size]]
        hb[idx] = 1.0
    return hb


def make_starts(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng):
    starts = []
    if parent_h is not None and np.asarray(parent_h).size > 0:
        parent_h = np.asarray(parent_h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        pn = parent_h.size
        xp = (np.arange(pn) + 0.5) * 2.0 / pn
        xn = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / n
        h0 = np.interp(xn, xp, parent_h, left=parent_h[0], right=parent_h[-1])
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
        starts.append(h0)

        hsym = 0.5 * (h0 + h0[::-1])
        starts.append(project_feasible(hsym, n))

        if n % 2 == 0:
            starts.append(rounded_binary(h0, n))

        starts.append(project_feasible(h0 + 0.04 * rng.randn(n), n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(h0 + 0.09 * rng.randn(n), n))
    else:
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / n
        c1 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0))
        c2 = 0.5 + 0.42 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0)) + 0.08 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * (x - 1.0))
        starts.append(project_feasible(c1, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(c2, n))
        starts.append(project_feasible(c1 + 0.05 * rng.randn(n), n))
        if n % 2 == 0:
            hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            hb[rng.permutation(n)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
            starts.append(hb)

    out = []
    for h in starts:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        out.append(h)
        if len(out) >= 5:
            break
    return out


def optimize_at_n(n, starts, time_limit, seed):
    t_start = time.time()
    if not starts:
        return None, float("inf")

    h_best = np.asarray(starts[0], dtype=float).copy()
    c_best = compute_c5(h_best)

    # A short SLSQP purification from a few good starts.
    fracs = [0.25, 0.12]
    caps = [120.0, 60.0]
    for i in range(min(2, len(starts))):
        elapsed = time.time() - t_start
        if time_limit - elapsed < 25.0:
            break
        t_s = min((time_limit - elapsed) * fracs[i], caps[i])
        if t_s < 5.0:
            continue
        h1 = slsqp_polish(starts[i], n, t_s, seed + 100 * i)
        c1 = compute_c5(h1)
        if c1 < c_best:
            c_best = c1
            h_best = h1.copy()

    # Coordinate descent is the main local search driver.
    elapsed = time.time() - t_start
    if time_limit - elapsed > 30.0:
        t_c = min((time_limit - elapsed) * 0.55, 500.0)
        if t_c < 20.0:
            t_c = time_limit - elapsed - 5.0
        if t_c > 5.0:
            h2, c2 = coordinate_optimize(h_best, n, t_c, seed + 2000)
            if c2 < c_best:
                c_best = c2
                h_best = h2.copy()

        elapsed = time.time() - t_start
        if time_limit - elapsed > 20.0:
            t_s = min(60.0, (time_limit - elapsed) * 0.18)
            if t_s > 5.0:
                h3 = slsqp_polish(h_best, n, t_s, seed + 3000)
                c3 = compute_c5(h3)
                if c3 < c_best:
                    c_best = c3
                    h_best = h3.copy()

        elapsed = time.time() - t_start
        if time_limit - elapsed > 20.0:
            t_c = min(180.0, (time_limit - elapsed) * 0.35)
            if t_c > 10.0:
                h4, c4 = coordinate_optimize(h_best, n, t_c, seed + 4000)
                if c4 < c_best:
                    c_best = c4
                    h_best = h4.copy()

    # Try two alternative basins from other starts.
    s_idx = []
    if len(starts) > 1:
        s_idx.append(1)
    if len(starts) > 2:
        s_idx.append(2)

    for it, s in enumerate(s_idx):
        elapsed = time.time() - t_start
        if time_limit - elapsed < 30.0:
            break
        frac = 0.22 if it == 0 else 0.12
        t_c = min((time_limit - elapsed) * frac, 160.0)
        if t_c < 15.0:
            continue
        h2, c2 = coordinate_optimize(starts[s], n, t_c, seed + 5000 + it)
        if c2 < c_best:
            c_best = c2
            h_best = h2.copy()

    # Final small polish at this resolution.
    elapsed = time.time() - t_start
    if time_limit - elapsed > 25.0:
        t_s = min(50.0, (time_limit - elapsed) * 0.5)
        if t_s > 5.0:
            h3 = slsqp_polish(h_best, n, t_s, seed + 6000)
            c3 = compute_c5(h3)
            if c3 < c_best:
                c_best = c3
                h_best = h3.copy()

    final_c = compute_c5(h_best)
    return h_best, final_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _g_n, _g_K
    t0 = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        ph = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
        if ph.size > 0:
            ph = project_feasible(ph, ph.size)
            best_h = ph
            best_n = len(ph)
            best_c = compute_c5(ph)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 192
        x = (np.arange(best_n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / best_n
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0))
        best_h = project_feasible(h0, best_n)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
        best_n = len(best_h)

    # Resolution choices: mostly near the parent, plus a few alternatives.
    base_n = max(96, int(best_n))
    if base_n % 2 != 0:
        base_n += 1

    priority = [
        int(best_n),
        base_n,
        base_n + 4,
        base_n + 8,
        base_n + 32,
        base_n + 64,
        256,
    ]
    fallback = [192, 224, 256, 288, 320]

    n_list = []
    seen = set()
    for val in priority + fallback:
        if val < 0:
            continue
        if val % 2 != 0:
            val += 1
        if 96 <= val <= 384 and val not in seen:
            seen.add(val)
            n_list.append(val)
        if len(n_list) >= 5:
            break
    if not n_list:
        n_list = [base_n]

    max_times = [600.0, 300.0, 180.0, 100.0, 60.0]
    for i, n in enumerate(n_list):
        elapsed = time.time() - t0
        rem = budget_s - elapsed - 40.0
        if rem < 40.0:
            break
        t_n = min(rem, max_times[i] if i < len(max_times) else 50.0)
        if t_n < 30.0:
            t_n = rem
        if t_n <= 5.0:
            break

        starts = make_starts(best_h, best_n, n, rng)
        h_loc, c_loc = optimize_at_n(n, starts, t_n, seed + 10000 * i)
        if h_loc is not None and c_loc < best_c:
            best_c = c_loc
            best_n = n
            best_h = h_loc.copy()

    # Heavy final polishing of the global best.
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    rem = budget_s - elapsed - 25.0
    if rem > 30.0:
        t1 = min(180.0, rem * 0.45)
        h1, c1 = coordinate_optimize(best_h, best_n, t1, seed + 20000)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    rem = budget_s - elapsed - 20.0
    if rem > 25.0:
        t2 = min(90.0, rem * 0.45)
        h2 = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, t2, seed + 21000)
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    rem = budget_s - elapsed - 15.0
    if rem > 20.0:
        t3 = min(90.0, rem * 0.7)
        h3, c3 = coordinate_optimize(best_h, best_n, t3, seed + 22000)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_h = h3.copy()

    # Refinements by symmetrization and thresholding / rounding.
    sym = project_feasible(0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]), best_n)
    cs = compute_c5(sym)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_c = cs
        best_h = sym

    if best_n % 2 == 0:
        for th in (0.50, 0.47, 0.53, 0.44, 0.56, 0.40, 0.60):
            hb = threshold_binary(best_h, best_n, th)
            cb = compute_c5(hb)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_h = hb

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ec5 = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if np.isfinite(ec5) and ec5 < final_c and (final_c - ec5) < 1e-8:
            final_c = float(ec5)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, final_c, best_n
```
